Top-level effecting statements pin their module (Module.has_side_effects). Over-removal = correctness bug, not an optimization.
  • Ownership/mutation: shake takes &mut ModuleGraph to record reachability/prune-decisions as metadata; AST node removal happens later in codegen against ReachableSet (graph stays the source of truth). Runs after the graph is built, before chunking — single-pass over a frozen-shape graph (only marker fields mutate).
  • Concurrency: marking is a worklist over the whole graph; runs once on the orchestrator thread (cross-module). Output ReachableSet is then read-only Send + Sync.

11.9 bndl-chunk — code splitting

pub struct ChunkId(pub u32);

pub enum OutputFormat { Esm, Cjs, Iife }

pub struct Chunk {
    pub id: ChunkId,
    pub modules: Vec<ModuleId>,        // in deterministic order
    pub entry: Option<ModuleId>,       // Some -> entry chunk
    pub is_dynamic: bool,              // produced by a dynamic import()
    pub hash: u64,                     // content hash (fixed-point across xchunk refs)
    pub imports: Vec<ChunkId>,         // cross-chunk edges
}

pub struct ChunkGraph { pub chunks: Vec<Chunk> }   // indexed by ChunkId

pub fn split(graph: &ModuleGraph, reachable: &ReachableSet, fmt: OutputFormat) -> ChunkGraph;
  • Algorithm (binding): esbuild/Rollup module groups — a module's chunk is keyed by the set of entry points (static + dynamic) that can reach it. Reachable-from-one → that entry's chunk; reachable-from-many → shared chunk. Dynamic import() = chunk boundary (is_dynamic).
  • Determinism: module order within a chunk and chunk naming are stable; hash is hash of final chunk content with a fixed-point pass so cross-chunk hash references converge (required for long-term CDN caching). u64 chunk hash distinct from the BLAKE3 CacheKey (§11.12).
  • Ownership/concurrency: split takes &ModuleGraph + &ReachableSet (read-only) → owns a fresh ChunkGraph. Pure function of its inputs; the ChunkGraph is then read-only for codegen.
  • Compatibility: OutputFormat #[non_exhaustive].

11.10 bndl-codegen — scope, rename, emit, minify

/// Scopes, bindings, references, scope ids. Built by scope analysis (Phase 8).
pub struct ScopeTree { /* scopes, bindings, refs, parent links */ }

/// Scope-aware rename map; produces collision-free names (short names for minify).
pub struct Renamer { /* per-scope rename table, used-name reservation */ }

/// Writer + indent + a source-map builder handle from project #265.
pub struct Emitter { /* out: String, indent, map_builder: SourceMapBuilder /*#265*/ */ }

/// mangle + DCE + fold passes over an AST already lowered/transformed.
pub struct Minifier { /* mangle: bool, fold: bool, dce: bool */ }

pub struct OutputChunk {
    pub file_name: String,
    pub code: String,
    pub map: Option<SourceMap>,        // #265 codec; bundler never hand-rolls VLQ
}

pub fn emit_chunk(chunk: &Chunk, graph: &ModuleGraph, fmt: OutputFormat,
                  opts: &CodegenOptions) -> OutputChunk;
  • Scope hoisting (binding): concatenate ESM modules into one scope when sound (no eval, no top-level CJS, not in a wrapper-forcing cycle); otherwise wrap in a runtime fn with first-call memoization. Renamer makes cross-module names collision-free and scope-correct while preserving live-binding identity.
  • Module runtime (injected): __require / __esm / __commonJS / __toESM / __export interop helpers bridge CJS↔ESM; OutputFormat selects ESM / CJS / IIFE wrappers.
  • Source maps: Emitter composes per-stage position deltas (lex→parse→transform→print→minify) into the final map via the #265 codec. SourceMap type is owned by #265 — referenced, not re-specified here.
  • Ownership/concurrency: emit_chunk is a pure function of (&Chunk, &ModuleGraph, fmt, &opts) → owns one OutputChunk; chunks emit in parallel across workers (graph is shared &, Send + Sync). Emitter/Renamer/Minifier are per-chunk, per-thread, owned.
  • Persistence: OutputChunk is the serializable build artifact (written to disk; cached as Artifact::Emitted). file_name includes the content hash.
  • Mutation: Minifier may mutate the per-chunk AST copy (mangle/DCE/fold) but never the shared frozen Module AST — it works on a clone/region scoped to the chunk.

11.11 bndl-cache — content-addressed store

/// BLAKE3 of (source content + relevant options). Equal key => reuse artifact.
pub struct CacheKey(pub [u8; 32]);

/// In-memory + on-disk content-addressed store. Thread-safe.
pub struct Cache { /* mem: ShardedMap<CacheKey, Artifact>, dir: PathBuf */ }

pub enum Artifact { Parsed(Program), Transformed(Program), Emitted(OutputChunk) }
  • Keying: CacheKey = BLAKE3 over normalized inputs (file bytes + the options that affect the stage: Syntax, Target, DefineMap digest, OutputFormat). Same key ⇒ byte-identical result (relies on §11.1 determinism).
  • Memoization: wraps parse / transform / codegen — Artifact variants mirror the three cacheable stages. Parsed/Transformed cache a Program; Emitted caches an OutputChunk.
  • Persistence: two-tier — in-memory shard for the live build, on-disk store for incremental rebuilds across runs. Change-driven invalidation: a changed file ⇒ new CacheKey ⇒ recompute that node + dirty dependents only.
  • Concurrency: Cache is Send + Sync; &self get/put from all workers (sharded interior mutability). Persistence (Program serialization) must round-trip spans/NodeId/Atom deterministically.

11.12 bndl-server — dev server & HMR

/// http + ws, owns graph + transform pipeline + cache + watcher.
pub struct DevServer { /* listener, graph: ModuleGraph, cache: Cache, watcher: Watcher */ }

/// Boundary computation + accepted-modules tracking.
pub struct HmrEngine { /* boundaries, accepted: Set<ModuleId> */ }

pub struct HmrBoundary { pub module: ModuleId, pub accepts: bool }

/// fs events -> invalidation signals into the graph + cache.
pub struct Watcher { /* fs watch, debounce, ModuleId mapping */ }
  • Model: Vite-style ESM-native on-demand transform (no upfront bundle); ModuleRunner (client) requests modules, server transforms+caches per request. Dependency pre-bundling collapses node_modules into prebuilt ESM.
  • HMR: on change, HmrEngine walks importers up to the nearest accepting HmrBoundary (accepts == true); if none, full-reload fallback. Propagation + the websocket HMR protocol + client runtime are Phase 12.
  • Ownership/concurrency: DevServer owns long-lived state; serves requests concurrently (graph + cache are Send + Sync, §11.6/§11.11). Watcher runs on its own thread, pushes invalidations.
  • Persistence: reuses bndl-cache on-disk store for warm restarts.

11.13 bndl-cli — plugin host

/// Hooks fire in order: resolve -> load -> transform -> render_chunk.
/// Each returns Option: Some short-circuits the default for that stage.
pub trait Plugin {
    fn resolve(&self, /* specifier, importer, opts */) -> Option<ResolveResult>;
    fn load(&self, /* path */) -> Option<String>;
    fn transform(&self, /* code, path, program */) -> Option<Program>;
    fn render_chunk(&self, /* chunk, code */) -> Option<OutputChunk>;
}

/// Subcommands: build serve transform analyze graph.
pub fn run(args: CliArgs) -> ExitCode;
  • Hook semantics: resolve overrides bndl-resolver (returns a ResolveResult); load supplies source for non-fs/virtual modules; transform returns a rewritten Program fed into the §11.7 pipeline; render_chunk post-processes an OutputChunk (§11.10).
  • Ownership/concurrency: plugins are registered once in the host; hooks must be Send + Sync (called from parallel workers) and side-effect-free w.r.t. shared state except via returned values. First plugin returning Some wins (ordered host).
  • Compatibility: Plugin is the public extension contract; hook signatures are frozen API. run maps subcommands to the orchestrator pipeline (resolve+load → graph → shake → chunk → emit).

11.14 Type ownership & lifecycle summary

Type Home crate Owned by Mutable when Send+Sync after build Serialized
Token, Lexer bndl-lexer parser worker lexing n/a (per-thread) no
Program/Expr/Stmt bndl-ast Module (then frozen) transform only yes (inside Module) via Artifact
Parser, ParseError bndl-parser worker parsing n/a errors: ephemeral
Resolver bndl-resolver Arc, shared never (interior cache) yes no
Module, ModuleGraph bndl-graph graph owns modules build only → frozen yes partial (cache)
TransformContext bndl-transform worker per pass no (per-thread) no
ReachableSet bndl-treeshake orchestrator shake only yes no
ChunkGraph, Chunk bndl-chunk orchestrator split only yes manifest
OutputChunk bndl-codegen emit result construction yes yes (disk + cache)
Cache, Artifact bndl-cache DevServer/orchestrator get/put (sharded) yes yes (on-disk)
DevServer, HmrEngine bndl-server process request handling yes warm cache only
Plugin bndl-cli host never (hooks &self) yes (required) no

All public enums above are #[non_exhaustive] (additive evolution); all numeric id newtypes are #[repr(transparent)] u32. Atom/Span/id-newtypes are Copy. The SourceMap format itself is defined by project #265 and only referenced here.

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton, workspace, and the "bundle two files into one" smoke test

0.1 Objectives

  • Stand up the Cargo workspace (12 bndl-* members, edition 2021, MSRV 1.78) with a green pipeline: fmt + clippy -D warnings + build + test.
  • Define compiling type shells only (no logic): Token/TokenKind/Span/SourceId; AST Program/ModuleItem/Stmt/Expr/Ident; ParseError/ParseErrorKind; ResolveError; ModuleId/Module/ModuleGraph; Chunk/OutputFormat; TransformContext; Plugin trait.
  • Ship one end-to-end vertical slice: bndl build entry.js where entry.js does import {x} from "./b.js" and b.js does export const x = ... → single ESM OutputChunk that runs under Node and prints the expected value.
  • Establish the differential-oracle scaffold: esbuild/Rollup invoked behind a dev-oracle cargo feature, never a runtime dep.
  • Lock determinism scaffolding: identical inputs+opts → byte-identical output (assert in smoke test).

0.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Step Module/file Content (shells only)
1 Cargo.toml (root) [workspace] members=[12 bndl-*], resolver="2", shared [workspace.package] version/edition/rust-version=1.78, [workspace.dependencies] (blake3, memchr, criterion, insta dev-only)
2 rust-toolchain.toml channel="1.78.0", components=[rustfmt,clippy]
3 bndl-lexer/src/lib.rs Span{start:u32,end:u32}, SourceId(u32), TokenKind (full variant set, locked), Token{kind,span,had_newline_before}, Lexer<'a> shell
4 bndl-ast/src/lib.rs NodeId(u32), Atom(=Box<str> placeholder, intern later), Program{items,source_type}, SourceType{Module,Script}, ModuleItem, Stmt, Expr, Ident, ImportDecl, ExportDecl; trait Visit/VisitMut (default no-op methods)
5 bndl-parser/src/lib.rs Syntax{typescript,jsx}, Parser<'a> shell, ParseError{kind,span}, ParseErrorKind (locked variants), parse_program(src,id,syntax)->(Program,Vec<ParseError>) → minimal hand-rolled ESM-only parse for the slice
6 bndl-resolver/src/lib.rs Condition, ResolveOptions, Specifier, ResolveResult, ResolveError, Resolver shell → relative-path + .js probing only
7 bndl-graph/src/lib.rs ModuleId(u32), ModuleKind, SymbolId, Symbol, ImportRecord, ImportKind, ExportRecord, Module, ModuleGraph shells
8 bndl-transform/src/lib.rs Target, DefineMap, JsxRuntime, TransformContext<'a>, trait TransformPass (no passes yet)
9 bndl-treeshake,bndl-chunk,bndl-codegen,bndl-cache,bndl-server lib.rs with locked struct/fn shells (shake, split, emit_chunk, CacheKey([u8;32]), DevServer) — unimplemented!()/identity stubs
10 bndl-chunk ChunkId(u32), OutputFormat{Esm,Cjs,Iife}, Chunk, ChunkGraph shells
11 bndl-cli/src/main.rs+lib.rs clap-free arg parse, CliArgs, subcommands build serve transform analyze graph, trait Plugin{resolve,load,transform,render_chunk}, run(args)->ExitCode; only build wired
12 bndl-cli orchestrator (build) entries→Resolver→read file→parse_program→insert Module into ModuleGraph→follow ImportRecord→identity transform→emit_chunk concat→write dist/ + manifest — the pipeline shape mirrors P1 (single-pass, parallel-per-file)
13 CI/infra .github/workflows/ci.yml (fmt,clippy -D,build,test,deny), Makefile (build test lint fmt fix oracle), Dockerfile (rust:1.78-slim + node for oracle), deny.toml (license/advisory allowlist) — ADR-27 gates every PR on fmt+clippy+build+test+deny
14 Oracle xtask or tests/oracle.rs behind #[cfg(feature="dev-oracle")]; shells out to npx esbuild/rollup, diff stdout-of-run not byte output (Phase 0) — ADR-24 differential oracle; ADR-01 Rust workspace enforced by ADR-02

0.3 Deliverable

bndl build tests/fixtures/smoke/entry.js -o dist/ produces dist/entry.<hash>.js (single ESM chunk) that, run via node dist/entry.<hash>.js, prints 42. Workspace compiles; make lint test is green; oracle scaffold runs under --features dev-oracle.

0.5 Conformance Fixtures

0.6 Pitfalls

  • Workspace resolver="2" omitted → feature unification leaks dev-oracle into release builds; symptom: oracle deps in cargo tree --no-default-features. Fix: resolver 2 + audit cargo tree.
  • Cyclic crate deps (e.g. graph↔transform via convenience re-export) → workspace won't compile. Root cause: shoving helpers in wrong crate. Keep the locked dep DAG; debug with cargo-depgraph.
  • Atom as String clone-everywhere baked into shells → costly to swap for interner in Phase 1. Use type Atom alias now so the change is one line.
  • Span as usize → non-portable map offsets, breaks #265 codec contract. Lock u32 byte offsets from day 1; assert source <4 GiB.
  • Oracle as normal dependency → ships node toolchain into the binary, bloats Docker, breaks cargo install. Must be #[cfg(feature)] + dev-only; CI gate that default build has zero oracle symbols.
  • Smoke test asserts byte-equality vs esbuild → false failures (cosmetic differences). Phase 0 oracle compares runtime stdout, not bytes.

0.7 Performance Concerns

  • No hot path yet; budget is build time: full workspace cold build <90 s on CI runner; incremental <5 s. Target informs MSRV/dep choices (avoid heavy proc-macro deps).
  • Establish criterion harness skeleton (empty benches) so Phase 1+ adds benches without churn.
  • Keep crate count of compile units lean; avoid serde_derive in core crates (compile-time tax) — defer to bndl-cache/bndl-cli only.

0.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty entry.js → empty but valid ESM chunk, exit 0.
  • entry.js with no imports → single-module chunk, no resolver edges.
  • Self-import import "./entry.js" → graph dedups by realpath ModuleId, no infinite loop.
  • BOM / CRLF in source → stripped/normalized before lexing; Span offsets account for it.
  • Specifier "./b" (no ext) → extension probing finds b.js; "./b.js" exact also works.

0.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
Resolve miss Resolver → ResolveError::NotFound structured diag (specifier+importer), exit 1, no partial dist/
Parse error Vec<ParseError> non-empty print all w/ Span carets, exit 1
Output dir unwritable io::Error on write wrap w/ path context, exit 2
Non-deterministic output CI double-build diff fail CI; bisect ordering (HashMap→BTreeMap/sorted vecs)
Panic on hot path catch_unwind at CLI boundary in debug abort w/ "internal error, file bug" + backtrace

0.10 Operational Notes

  • make targets: build test lint fmt fix oracle bench docker; CI mirrors them 1:1.
  • Versioning: workspace-inherited version (start 0.0.0); SemVer applies post-Phase 13 gate.
  • deny.toml pins allowed licenses (MIT/Apache-2.0/BSD) + RUSTSEC advisory DB check in CI.
  • Docker: multi-stage rust:1.78-slim builder → distroless runtime; node only in the oracle stage.
  • Logging: RUST_LOG-style env via lightweight log facade; default WARN, -v flags bump per crate.
  • Non-goals reminder (do not creep): no type checking (#42), no JS execution (#126), no source-map format (consume #265), no installer (#135).

13. Phase 1 — ECMAScript / TS / JSX lexer

Implements bndl-lexer in full: byte-string → Token stream per the ECMAScript lexical grammar, with TS/JSX token shapes, ASI tracking, template-state stack, and regex-vs-divide disambiguation. Total function over any &[u8] (incl. invalid UTF-8, NUL, unterminated) → finite stream ending in Eof; never panics, recovers via localized Invalid tokens.

1.1 Objectives

  • Cover ES2022 lexical grammar: IdentifierName, Keyword, PrivateName (#x), NumericLiteral (dec/hex/oct/bin + _ separators + legacy octal), BigInt (n suffix), StringLiteral, template tokens (NoSubTemplate/Head/Middle/Tail), RegularExpressionLiteral, all punctuators.
  • Track had_newline_before on every Token → feeds parser ASI (Phase 2). Resolve regex-vs-/ via parser-fed regex_context flag (lexer cannot decide alone).
  • Emit TS-relevant tokens (?, !, </>, type punct reuse existing kinds) and JSX shapes (JsxText, JsxIdent) gated by parser mode, not lexer config.

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module (crates/bndl-lexer/src/) Adds
span.rs Span{start,end:u32} (byte offsets), SourceId(u32); helper Span::new, len, merge
token.rs TokenKind, Token{kind,span,had_newline_before}, Keyword, Punct enums
classify.rs code-point classifier: is_id_start/is_id_continue (Unicode ID_Start/ID_Continue ranges + $/_), is_ws, is_line_terminator (LF/CR/U+2028/U+2029), is_dec/hex/oct/bin_digit
lexer.rs Lexer<'a>{src:&'a [u8], pos:u32, source_id, regex_context:bool, template_stack:Vec<TemplateState>, jsx_mode:bool}; main next_token dispatch
number.rs numeric/bigint scan: radix prefix, separator rules, f64 parse, BigInt token (lexer stores span only)
string.rs string + escape decoding shared with template cooking: \n \t \\ \xNN \uNNNN \u{...}, line-continuation, octal-in-string (sloppy only)
template.rs template DFA + TemplateState stack frame
jsx.rs next_jsx_text, next_jsx_ident entry points (parser-driven)

Core decode: UTF-8 decode on demand at pos; invalid sequence → consume 1 byte, mark span Invalid, continue (B6 never-panic contract; ADR-03 hand-written recovery); Atom interning deferred to parser, lexer yields spans only (sym resolved by slicing src[span]). Dispatch in next_token: skip trivia (WS/line-terminators/comments/hashbang) recording newline → set had_newline_before (S1 ASI restricted productions; B6 ASI hazards); match first code point: id-start→ident/keyword/private; digit→number; "/'→string; `→template; /→regex-or-punct (regex_context — ADR-03 parser-fed disambiguation; B2 maximal-munch); else→punctuator (maximal-munch trie; S12 UAX#31 ID_Start/ID_Continue).

EBNF (lexer-relevant excerpt):

Token        ::= IdentifierName | PrivateName | Punctuator | NumericLiteral
               | StringLiteral  | Template     | RegExp     | TemplatePart
IdentifierName ::= IdStart IdContinue*
PrivateName  ::= "#" IdentifierName
NumericLiteral ::= ( "0x" HexDigits | "0o" OctDigits | "0b" BinDigits
                   | DecimalLiteral ) "n"?
Sep          ::= "_"                       (* not leading/trailing/double *)

Template DFA (template_stack holds one TemplateState per open ` / ${):

state TplString:  read chars; on "`" -> emit NoSubTemplate|TemplateTail, pop
                  on "${"        -> emit TemplateHead|TemplateMiddle, push Expr
state Expr (normal lexing): on "}" with top==Expr -> pop, re-enter TplString
                            tracked by counting "{"/"}" so object literals inside ${} nest

Regex-vs-divide: parser sets regex_context=true after positions where a / must begin a regex (after (, ,, =, return, [, operators, start-of-input) and false after an operand (ident, ), ], number, string, template-tail); on / with regex_context, scan RegularExpressionBody honoring [...] class (slashes inside class not terminators), then RegularExpressionFlags — else emit / or /=.

1.3 Deliverable

bndl-lexer crate: Lexer::new(src,id) -> Lexer, Lexer::next_token() -> Token, iterator tokens(); round-trips (concat of token source-slices + trivia == input); cargo test -p bndl-lexer green; fuzz target fuzz_lex builds.

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Test262 lexical subset (tokenizable programs) 100% no-panic; ≥99.5% kind-correct vs golden
Round-trip (src reconstructable from spans+trivia) byte-exact, all fixtures
Panic/abort on arbitrary &[u8] 0 (≥10M fuzz iters, ASAN clean)
Throughput, single thread ≥40 MB/s ASCII-heavy JS (Phase-4 target reuse)
had_newline_before correctness matches reference on ASI corpus, 100%

1.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expects
numbers.js 1_000, 0xFF, 0b1010, 1e10, .5, 9007199254740993n → Num/BigInt, separators stripped in value
templates.js `a${b}c${`d${e}`}f` → Head/Middle/Tail + nested push/pop, balanced stack at EOF
regex_vs_div.js a/b/g (divide) vs return /ab/g vs [/x/] → context-correct Regex/Punct(Slash)
unicode_id.js café, \u{1F600} (invalid id), π, $_ → Ident vs Invalid
private.ts #x, this.#y, #x in o → PrivateName
malformed.js "unterminated, `open, 0xG, 1__2, \uXYZ, lone surrogate, raw NUL, invalid UTF-8 → localized Invalid, stream still ends Eof
comments.js // line, /* block */, #!/usr/bin/env node (only col 0 line 1) → skipped, newline flag set

1.6 Pitfalls

  • } ambiguity in templates — } may close a ${...} substitution OR an object literal inside it. Root cause: shared } token. Fix: brace-depth counter per Expr frame; only depth-0 } pops to TplString. Debug: dump template_stack on each }.
  • Regex context staleness — parser forgets to update regex_context after a token → /=/ mis-lexed. Symptom: Punct where Regex expected. Debug: assert on transition table in tests; log (prev_kind, regex_context).
  • Unicode-escape in identifiers — abc is identifier abc; escape must decode to a valid IdStart/IdContinue, else Invalid (not silent accept). Root cause: skipping escape validation. Debug: fixture unicode_id.js.
  • Legacy octal vs separator — 0o17 (modern) vs 017 (legacy octal, sloppy-only) vs 0_1 (illegal). Conflating → wrong value/strict-mode bug. Lexer tags legacy-octal so parser can reject in strict mode.
  • CR-LF newline double-count — \r\n is ONE line terminator for ASI; counting two corrupts column maps. Treat \r\n atomically.
  • Maximal munch on ?. — a?.5 is a ? .5 (cond + number) NOT optional-chain (?. not followed by digit). Lexer peeks: ?. only if next not a digit.

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • ASCII fast path: branch on byte < 0x80 before UTF-8 decode; only decode when high bit set → avoids per-char char cost on typical source.
  • No allocation in hot loop: lexer holds &[u8] + cursor; no String per token; interning deferred; template_stack is the only heap, reused (clear not realloc). Punctuator trie inlined as nested match (LUT-style) → branch-predictable; avoids hashmap.
  • Target ≥40 MB/s/core; Token kept ≤16 bytes (TokenKind niche-packed, Span 8B, bool 1B) for cache density.

1.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty input → single Eof, had_newline_before=false.
  • BOM (U+FEFF) at start → skipped as WS; mid-file ZWNBSP → WS.
  • <!-- / --> HTML-like comments (Annex B, Script only) → line comment; gated off in Module mode.
  •  /  line/para separators are line terminators (affect ASI) but legal inside strings.
  • Numeric edge: 1., .5, 1e+10, 0., leading-zero 08 (legacy, decimal in strict), 1n.0 illegal.
  • Astral identifiers (U+10000+) via surrogate-free UTF-8; \u{...} > 0x10FFFF → Invalid.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Unterminated string/template hit EOF/line-term before close emit Invalid span to EOF/line, set ParseErrorKind::Unterminated* hint, resync at next line
Invalid escape (\uXYZ) escape decoder rejects Invalid token over escape, continue scanning literal body
Bad number (0xG, 1__2) radix/separator validator fails consume the numeric run, emit Invalid, do not stall
Invalid UTF-8 / lone surrogate decoder error skip 1 byte, Invalid, advance (guarantees termination)
Unbalanced ${/} at EOF template_stack non-empty at Eof flush remaining frames as Invalid, still emit Eof; invariant: every next_token advances pos by ≥1 (or returns Eof) → loop always terminates

1.10 Operational Notes

  • Determinism: pure function of (src, regex_context, jsx_mode); no globals, no time/env → byte-identical token stream across runs (feeds content-addressed cache, Phase 13).
  • Spans are byte offsets; consumers slice src[span] for text. Source-map column derivation deferred to #265 codec.
  • bndl-cli analyze --tokens <file> dumps the stream (kind, span, newline-flag) for debugging.
  • RUST_LOG=bndl_lexer=trace traces template-stack and regex-context transitions.
  • Fuzz (cargo fuzz run fuzz_lex) + round-trip property test wired into CI; ASAN/Miri pass required to merge.

14. Phase 2 — Recursive-descent + Pratt parser (ES2022 core)

Builds bndl-parser core: parse_program turns a bndl-lexer token stream into an ESTree-shaped, span-tagged Program. Recursive descent for statements/declarations; Pratt for expressions; full ES2022 (no TS/JSX yet — Phase 3). Every node carries Span + NodeId. Errors recover at statement boundaries; parser never panics. TS/JSX token shapes already lex (Phase 1) but are gated off via Syntax{typescript:false, jsx:false}.

2.1 Objectives

  • Statements/declarations via recursive descent; expressions via Pratt (binding powers, associativity, comma/assignment/conditional ladder).
  • Patterns + destructuring (array/object, defaults, rest); cover-grammar reparse Expr -> Pat.
  • Classes: fields, private members (#x), static blocks, computed keys, getters/setters, super.
  • async/await, generators (function*, yield/yield*), async arrows.
  • ES modules: import/export (named/default/namespace/* as ns), import() dynamic, import.meta.
  • Attach Span (lexer-byte-accurate) + monotonically-increasing NodeId to every node.
  • ParseError recovery: synchronize at ;/}/statement-start keyword; emit Vec<ParseError>, return best-effort AST. Non-goal: type checking, TS/JSX bodies (Phase 3).

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files under crates/bndl-parser/src/:

File Contents
lib.rs parse_program(src,id,syntax)->(Program,Vec<ParseError>); constructs Parser, drives module/script item loop.
parser.rs Parser<'a>{ lexer, cur:Token, ahead:Option<Token>, syntax:Syntax, errors:Vec<ParseError>, node_ctr:u32, ctx:Ctx }; bump/peek/eat/expect/at; next_node_id(); span helpers start()/finish(lo).
stmt.rs recursive-descent: parse_stmt, parse_var_decl, parse_if/for/while/switch/try/return/..., block, labeled.
expr.rs Pratt parse_expr_bp(min_bp); parse_unary/postfix/primary; comma parse_expr (Sequence).
binding_power.rs infix_bp(Punct)->Option<(u8,u8)> left/right BP table; prefix_bp, postfix_bp.
pat.rs parse_binding_pat, expr_to_pat(Expr)->Result<Pat,ParseError> (cover-grammar).
class.rs parse_class: heritage, member loop (modifier flags static/async/get/set/*, private, computed), static block.
func.rs parse_function/parse_arrow; params via pat.rs; Ctx{in_async,in_gen,in_func} stack.
module.rs parse_import_decl, parse_export_decl -> ImportDecl/ExportDecl; import()/import.meta.
error.rs ParseError{kind:ParseErrorKind, span:Span, msg:&'static str}; recover_to_stmt_boundary().
ctx.rs Ctx bitflags: IN_ASYNC, IN_GEN, IN_LOOP, IN_SWITCH, NO_IN (for for-in disambiguation), STRICT.

Algorithms: (a) Pratt loop (B1 Ch.6, B2 §4.4, ADR-04) — fetch cur, parse prefix into lhs, while infix_bp(cur).0 >= min_bp recurse with right BP; assignment/?:/?? are right-assoc, binary left-assoc. (b) Cover grammar (S3 ES2022+ cover-grammar) — parse ( ... ) and { ... }/[ ... ] as Expr; on =>/= reinterpret via expr_to_pat, else keep Expr (no backtracking, single reparse). (c) ASI (S1 §12.9; B6 Appendix A/B) — on missing ;, accept if cur.had_newline_before, cur is }/Eof, or restricted-production (return/throw/break/continue/postfix ++/--). (d) NodeId allocated at node-start; Span{ lo, hi=prev_token_end } (ADR-05 ESTree-shaped, span-tagged).

2.3 Deliverable

parse_program parses arbitrary ES2022 modules + scripts into a correct ESTree-shaped Program (every node Span+NodeId), returning (Program, Vec<ParseError>) — never panics. Round-trip: AST printed by a debug pretty-printer re-parses to an equal AST (modulo spans). Passes hand-built suite + subset-Test262 (pass/+fail/).

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • All hand-built fixtures parse to expected AST shape (snapshot-tested); 0 panics across fuzz corpus.
  • Subset-Test262: ≥99% of curated pass/ accepted, ≥99% fail/ rejected with a ParseError (not panic/hang).
  • Spans byte-exact: &src[node.span.lo..node.span.hi] re-tokenizes to that node for 100% of sampled nodes.
  • NodeId unique + monotonic; ASI matches V8 on the ASI fixture set (≥40 cases).
  • Throughput ≥ 5 MB/s single-thread parse on the 50 KB React-build fixture; recovery yields ≥1 valid stmt after each injected error.

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expects
pratt_precedence.js a+b*c**d, a?b:c?d:e, a=b=c, ?? mix → exact tree (BP/assoc correct).
destructuring.js const{a,b:{c}=d,...r}=o; [x,,y=1,...z]=a → nested Pat with defaults/rest.
class_full.js fields, #priv, static{}, computed [k](), get/set, super.m() → ClassDecl members.
async_gen.js async function*f(){ yield* g(); await x }, async arrow → ctx flags + nodes.
modules.js named/default/namespace import+export, export * as ns, import(), import.meta → ImportDecl/ExportDecl.
asi.js restricted productions + newline cases → matches V8.
recover.js 3 injected syntax errors → 3 ParseError + ≥1 valid stmt each, no panic.
malformed/*.js ({a=1}) as expr (Invalid LHS), for(let of x), unterminated import {, await outside async (script) → specific ParseErrorKind.

2.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
{} ambiguity (block vs object) statement vs expr position dispatch on position: stmt-start { → block; else object via parse_expr.
(a,b)=> cover-grammar leak parsed as Sequence, then => defer; on => run expr_to_pat; reject (a,b,)=> trailing-comma-only-in-params.
Async-arrow vs call async(x) async is contextual lookahead: async+no-newline+(/Ident+=> → arrow; else call/ident.
Regex-vs-divide misclassified lexer needs parser context parser sets regex_allowed after operators/(/,/return; bug surfaces as InvalidRegex or stray Punct(Slash).
ASI swallowed restricted prod return\n expr check had_newline_before BEFORE consuming operand; insert ;.
Private-name scope #x outside class track class-stack; obj.#x only inside enclosing class → else ParseError.

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • Single-pass, no backtracking (cover-grammar = one reparse, O(n)); 1-token lookahead (ahead), peek is O(1).
  • AST nodes bump-allocated (arena from Phase 0); Box<Expr> only where recursive; Atom-interned idents (dedupe).
  • Target ≥5 MB/s; recursion depth-limited (≥512, configurable) → return ParseError not stack overflow on deep nesting.
  • Avoid per-node heap: Vec<ModuleItem> reserved by token-count heuristic; Span is 8 bytes (two u32).

2.8 Edge Cases

  • Trailing commas: calls/arrays/objects/params (yes), import{a,}/export{a,} (yes), only-comma params (no).
  • for(let x of y) vs for(x in y) vs for(;;) — NO_IN ctx; let as ident in sloppy mode.
  • yield/await as identifier when not in gen/async (sloppy script) vs reserved in module/strict.
  • Optional chaining short-circuit a?.b.c, a?.(), a?.[k]; new precedence (new a.b(), new new a()()).
  • Numeric separators, BigInt, octal-in-sloppy; directive prologue "use strict" flips STRICT.
  • Empty program, lone ;, label on iteration vs break/continue target validity.

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
Unexpected token expect mismatch push UnexpectedToken{span}; recover_to_stmt_boundary; continue.
Invalid assignment target expr_to_pat fails on LHS of = ParseError; keep Assign node with placeholder pat.
Bad import/export module.rs shape check BadImport/BadExport; skip to ;.
await/yield misuse ctx-flag check ReservedWord; treat as ident in recovery.
Deep nesting depth counter > limit ParseError (no panic), bounded memory.
TS/JSX seen, flag off </type-annot in disabled syntax JsxNotEnabled/TrailingTsType.

2.10 Operational Notes

  • bndl-cli transform --no-emit / bndl analyze print AST (debug) + ParseError list for any file.
  • Snapshot tests via insta; AST debug fmt stable + span-stripped variant for round-trip eq.
  • Feature parser-trace logs the recursive-descent/Pratt call stack per token (debugging precedence/ASI).
  • Subset-Test262 runner vendored under tests/test262/; updated quarterly; pass/fail manifest checked in.
  • parse_program is the reuse boundary for Phase 5 (bndl-graph scan) + Phase 12 (bndl-server on-demand) — pure, Send-safe output.

15. Phase 3 — TypeScript + JSX parsing (parse-and-discard types)

Extends bndl-parser under Syntax{typescript,jsx}. Lex emits JsxText/JsxIdent/PrivateName; this phase adds the TS + JSX grammar productions, parsing them into spans and then discarding all type-only nodes — output is the same JS-only Program from Phase 2. No type checking (→ #42 Type Checker). Modeled on SWC/OXC TS+JSX parsers.

3.1 Objectives

  • Parse TS surface syntax: type annotations, interface, type aliases, enum/const enum, generics (<T> decls + call/type args), parameter properties, decorators, as/satisfies/<T>x, import type/export type, ambient declare, namespace/module.
  • Parse JSX: elements, fragments, attributes, spread attrs, expression containers, member/namespaced tag names; resolve the < JSX-vs-less-than/type-arg ambiguity; honor @jsxRuntime/@jsx/@jsxFrag pragma comments.
  • Strip all type-only constructs → AST identical in shape to a plain-JS parse (types collapse to Spans only; runtime nodes survive).
  • Gate every production behind Syntax{typescript,jsx}; mis-enabled syntax → ParseError, never a panic.
  • File-ext → default Syntax mapping for .ts/.tsx/.mts/.cts/.d.ts/.jsx; .d.ts parses then yields zero runtime items.

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files (under crates/bndl-parser/src/):

File Adds
ts/types.rs parse_ts_type (Pratt over type ops: `
ts/decl.rs interface, type alias, enum/const enum, declare, namespace/module, abstract class. Interfaces/aliases/declare → no ModuleItem (skipped). enum → desugars to runtime IIFE-object VarDecl.
ts/modifiers.rs param properties (public/private/protected/readonly/override on ctor params → synthesize field assign), accessor, definite-assign !, optional ?.
ts/decorator.rs @expr (+ call/member) before class/method/prop/param/accessor; emitted as real runtime Decorator exprs (not stripped).
ts/import_type.rs import type, export type, inline import { type X }; all elided from emitted import/export records.
jsx/element.rs JSXElement, JSXFragment, JSXOpening/Closing, member/namespaced names, self-closing; recursive children.
jsx/attr.rs JSXAttribute, JSXSpreadAttribute, JSXExpressionContainer, string/JsxText/empty-expr children.

Driver hooks (S4 TypeScript grammar; S5 JSX spec; R6 Babel ts+jsx plugins; ADR-04 recursive-descent+Pratt): Parser gains in_type_context: bool, ts_in_disallow_conditional, JSX child mode. parse_unary checks < → if syntax.jsx && lookahead-is-JSX → parse_jsx; elif syntax.typescript && type-arg-able → tentative type-arg parse w/ backtrack; else relational <. as/satisfies parsed as postfix at the assignment binding-power tier (lower than ||), strips to inner expr. Stripping rule (ADR-05 parse-and-discard TS; non-goal §1.2 boundary): type-only items return None from parse_module_item; type-only exprs (as/<T>/non-null !) return inner. New ParseErrorKind::{TrailingTsType, JsxNotEnabled} (already in spine; B5 error-recovery) used for gating.

3.3 Deliverable

parse_program(src, id, Syntax{typescript:true,jsx:true}) returns a JS-only Program for any .ts/.tsx/.mts/.cts/.d.ts/.jsx input. cargo run -p bndl-cli -- transform foo.tsx --syntax ts,jsx prints the type-stripped AST. enum and decorators survive as runtime; everything type-only is gone. .d.ts → empty items.

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 100% of the TS+JSX fixture suite parses with byte-identical post-strip AST vs the SWC oracle (span-normalized, node_id-erased compare).
  • Round-trip: strip-and-print of a TS file ≡ print of the same file with types hand-removed (DefinitelyTyped sample N≥200 .d.ts → 0 runtime items).
  • < ambiguity: 30-case table (JSX / type-arg-call / relational) all resolve correctly.
  • Mis-enabled: JSX with jsx:false → JsxNotEnabled; TS annotation with typescript:false → TrailingTsType; 0 panics under fuzz (10M iters).

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expects
enum_const.ts const enum E{A,B} → inlined-ready object IIFE VarDecl; reverse-mapped numeric members
param_props.ts constructor(private x:number) → ctor body gains this.x=x
decorators.tsx @Component() class + @Input() p + param decorator retained as runtime exprs
generics_call.ts f<T>(x), new Map<K,V>(), arrow <T,>()=>x (.tsx trailing-comma disambig)
as_satisfies.ts x as const, y satisfies T, <T>z all strip to inner
import_type.ts import type{A}, import{type B,c} → only c in import record
jsx_full.tsx element/fragment/spread-attr/{expr}/member-tag/namespaced-attr
pragma.jsx /* @jsx h */ /* @jsxFrag F */ recorded for Phase 6
ambient.d.ts parses → items.len()==0
Malformed unterminated_jsx.tsx (<a> no close → UnexpectedToken + recover), bad_type.ts (let x: =), jsx_no_close_frag.tsx, decorator_on_iface.ts

3.6 Pitfalls

  • < trilemma (a < b vs f<T>() vs <div>): root cause = unbounded lookahead. Fix: JSX wins when jsx set + next is ident/>; else tentative type-arg parse with checkpoint+backtrack; debug via --dump-token-context.
  • .tsx arrow generic: <T>()=>{} is JSX in .tsx; require <T,> or <T extends U>. Forgetting → spurious JSX parse. Debug: compare .ts vs .tsx Syntax flag.
  • ASI vs JSX/type: newline before < must not insert ; mid-JSX-child; had_newline_before only governs statement boundaries. Symptom: truncated JSX children.
  • enum is NOT pure-strip: it emits runtime; deleting it (like an interface) loses values. Reverse-mapping + const enum inlining are runtime semantics.
  • Decorators are runtime, types are not: stripping a decorator silently breaks frameworks. Keep Decorator nodes; only strip their type args.
  • import type elision: must remove from ImportRecord so bndl-graph doesn't pull a type-only dep (dead edge / wrong tree-shake). Inline { type X } filters per-specifier.

3.7 Performance Concerns

  • TS+JSX must add <15% parse time over plain JS; backtracking is the risk → cap with single-checkpoint tentative parse (no nested speculation), reuse lexer position, no token-vec copy.
  • Stripped type nodes allocate 0 heap (parse-to-span, no Box); arena untouched by discarded types.
  • JSX text: bulk-scan via lexer JsxText run, no per-char node. Target ≥10 MB/s on TSX-heavy input (React component files).

3.8 Edge Cases

  • a < b > c (relational) vs f<a>(b); x ? a : b vs conditional type; >>/>>> closing nested generics (re-split punct).
  • JSX: {/* comment */}, empty {} expr container (error), {...spread} attr, <>...</>, a.b.c / a:b tag names, entities &amp; in JsxText, < inside text.
  • TS: optional-method ?, definite !:, index signatures, overload signatures (multiple sig decls, single impl), declare global, triple-slash /// <reference> (recorded, not resolved).
  • export type * from, export { type X } from, export =/import x = require() (CJS-TS, kept as runtime).

3.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Unterminated JSX / type scan hits Eof mid-construct emit ParseError, sync to next stmt boundary, continue
Wrong < branch backtrack budget exhausted fall back to relational; record diagnostic
enum mis-strip fixture diff shows missing runtime regression gate in 3.4
Type leaks into emitted AST oracle diff non-empty assert no TS node kinds reachable post-parse
Panic on malformed TSX fuzzer Invalid token + error node, never unwrap

3.10 Operational Notes

  • Ext→Syntax: .ts/.mts/.cts/.d.ts→{ts:true,jsx:false}; .tsx→{ts:true,jsx:true}; .jsx→{ts:false,jsx:true}; CLI --syntax overrides; --jsx-pragma overrides pragma scan.
  • Diagnostics: bndl analyze --emit-stripped shows pre/post-strip diff; --keep-types (debug only) annotates spans.
  • Type checking is explicitly out of scope (→ #42); bundler trusts source, never validates types. Decorator legacy-vs-TC39 semantics deferred to Phase 6 lowering (this phase only parses).
  • All TS/JSX nodes carry Span for #265 source-map composition even though discarded.

16. Phase 4 — Module resolution (Node CJS + ESM)

4.1 Objectives

  • Implement bndl-resolver: Node CJS (LOAD_AS_FILE/LOAD_AS_DIRECTORY/LOAD_NODE_MODULES) + ESM (PACKAGE_RESOLVE/PACKAGE_EXPORTS_RESOLVE/PACKAGE_IMPORTS_RESOLVE/ESM_FILE_FORMAT) algorithms.
  • Match Node + tsc resolution byte-for-byte on a fixture matrix; emit ResolveResult{path, kind, side_effects, from_node_modules}.
  • Honor package.json exports/imports/main/module/browser/type/sideEffects with Condition matching + * patterns.
  • tsconfig paths/baseUrl applied before node_modules; symlink realpath; external + builtin classification; coherent multi-layer cache.
  • Non-goal: no install/fetch (#135), no type info from tsconfig (types parsed in #42).

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File (bndl-resolver/src/) Contents
specifier.rs Specifier classify: ./..//→Relative/Absolute; #→imports; node:/builtin set→Builtin; else Bare{pkg,sub} (scope-aware @scope/name split).
cjs.rs load_as_file(p) probe p, p.{js,json,node}, then resolver extensions; load_as_directory reads main→load_as_file/index.*; load_node_modules walks node_modules up dir chain.
esm.rs package_resolve→package_exports_resolve→package_target_resolve (string/array/object conditions, recursion); esm_file_format maps ext+nearest type to ModuleKind.
exports.rs conditional + * pattern matcher: longest-key + most-specific pattern; null target = blocked; imports.rs # internal resolve via PACKAGE_IMPORTS_RESOLVE.
pkg_json.rs PackageJson{type,main,module,browser,exports,imports,side_effects}; parse once, cache by realpath; browser map (string + false-shim + replace map).
tsconfig.rs TsConfig{base_url,paths,extends}; extends merge chain; glob-* path match longest-prefix; tried before bare resolution.
cache.rs fs_exists/dir_read/realpath/pkg_json caches keyed by canonical path; ResolveResult memo keyed (importer_dir, specifier, conditions_hash).
lib.rs Resolver::resolve(importer:&Path, spec:&str)->Result<ResolveResult,ResolveError>; dispatch on Specifier; final realpath + side_effects + kind.
  • Algorithm order (S6 Node ESM PACKAGE_RESOLVE; S7 Node CJS LOAD_AS_FILE/DIRECTORY/NODE_MODULES; S9 tsconfig paths; ADR-09 from-scratch for cache control): tsconfig paths → relative/absolute (CJS file/dir probe) → bare (exports if present else main/module/browser legacy) → node_modules walk → external/builtin.
  • ResolveOptions.main_fields ordered (["browser","module","main"] web; ["main","module"] node) selects legacy entry; conditions ordered list drives exports (S6 PACKAGE_EXPORTS_RESOLVE condition-match order; S8 package.json fields).

4.3 Deliverable

bndl-resolver crate: Resolver::new(opts) + resolve() returning ResolveResult; passes the fixture matrix (§4.5) against captured Node require.resolve/import.meta.resolve + tsc --traceResolution oracles. CLI bndl analyze --resolve <spec> --from <file> prints resolved path + kind + conditions hit.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Node CJS/ESM oracle match 100% on ≥400 fixture rows
tsconfig paths/baseUrl vs tsc --traceResolution 100% match
exports conditional + * pattern correctness all WG-resolve test vectors pass
Warm-cache resolve <2µs median; <50µs cold (1 stat-chain)
Determinism identical (importer,spec,opts)→identical result, order-independent
Errors correct ResolveError variant (no panic) on every malformed fixture

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
rel/a.js imports ./b → b.js (ext probe), kind=Esm if type:module
pkg-exports exports:{".":{"import":"./e.mjs","require":"./c.cjs"}} conditions pick mjs vs cjs
pkg-exports-star "./feat/*":"./src/*.js" pkg/feat/x→src/x.js
pkg-imports #dep→./impl.js internal # resolve
browser-shim "browser":{"./node.js":false} empty-module sentinel
tsconfig-paths "@app/*":["src/*"] @app/x→src/x.ts before node_modules
symlink-pnpm realpath dedup canonical path, from_node_modules=true
sideeffects-false side_effects=None
malformed: exports non-existent target ResolveError::NotExported
malformed: "./x" subpath not in exports NotExported (not fallback)
malformed: missing pkg PackageNotFound; bad paths glob

4.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause → debug
exports present silently disables deep imports spec: exports = full encapsulation; subpath not listed → hard NotExported, never legacy probe. Trace: log "exports gate" decision.
Condition order vs key order match is first matching condition in resolver's ordered list, not object key order; serde must preserve order (IndexMap).
main vs module precedence wrong controlled by main_fields ordering, not hardcoded; web vs node profiles differ.
Symlink identity breaks dedup/HMR resolve to realpath always; two specifiers→same realpath = one ModuleId. Debug: dump realpath cache.
* greedy vs first match pattern match picks longest matching key prefix + leftmost *; sort keys by specificity.
. extension on directory (./utils.js vs ./utils/) probe file first, then dir/index; missing-ext on dir = NotFound, common false bug.

4.7 Performance Concerns

  • Hot path = filesystem syscalls → cache stat/readdir/realpath; one node_modules walk amortized across siblings via shared parent cache.
  • package.json parsed once per realpath, IndexMap for exports/conditions; specifier classify is allocation-free (&str slices).
  • Resolution memo keyed (importer_dir, specifier, conditions_hash) → repeat imports O(1); concurrent resolves share RwLock/DashMap fs caches (Send+Sync).
  • Target: 50k specifiers resolved <100ms warm; cold dominated by syscalls, not CPU.

4.8 Edge Cases

  • node:-prefixed + bare builtins (fs, path) → ModuleKind::Builtin; unknown bare with no node_modules hit + external opt → ModuleKind::External.
  • Scoped @scope/pkg/sub split; trailing-slash specifier = directory exports "./" legacy.
  • exports sugar (string = "."); array fallback (try each until resolve); null = explicitly blocked.
  • browser field both forms: string replace (whole pkg) + object map (per-file, false=empty shim).
  • tsconfig extends chain (npm pkg + relative) merged base-up; baseUrl w/o paths = bare-relative-to-base.
  • Case-sensitivity: preserve as-written; warn on case-mismatch (macOS APFS hazard).
  • .cjs/.mjs force kind regardless of type; .ts/.tsx/.d.ts ext set added when TS enabled.

4.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
ResolveError::NotFound deep import exhausted file/dir/ext probes report tried-paths list in error span
InvalidExports (malformed exports value) non-string/array/object target error w/ pkg path; do not crash graph build
NotExported subpath blocked subpath absent from exports map distinct from NotFound; suggest valid subpaths
BadTsPaths glob with >1 * or no base reject at TsConfig load, fail fast
Symlink cycle in realpath depth guard (>40) ResolveError::NotFound + cycle note
Cache poisoning after fs change mtime/inval signal from #265/server Cache invalidate by path prefix; never serve stale

4.10 Operational Notes

  • ResolveOptions fully drives behavior (conditions, extensions, main_fields, ts_config, prefer_relative) → reproducible; serialize into CacheKey (BLAKE3) so resolution participates in incremental cache (#265 phase / bndl-cache).
  • Plugin resolve hook (Phase 13 Plugin trait) runs before built-in resolver; returns Option<ResolveResult> to override.
  • bndl analyze --resolve + --trace-resolution flag mirror tsc output for diffing oracles in CI.
  • Watch mode (bndl-server): Watcher fs events invalidate realpath + pkg_json caches by path; resolver re-runs on changed importer only.
  • Determinism contract: caches affect speed, never result; CI runs cold + warm and asserts identical ResolveResult.

17. Phase 5 — Module graph construction + live bindings

5.1 Objectives

  • Build bndl-graph: lift each parsed Program into a Module with scanned ImportRecord/ExportRecord/Symbol tables; assemble the ModuleId-keyed ModuleGraph.
  • Classify ModuleKind (Esm/Cjs/Json/Css/Asset/Builtin/External) per module; resolve every edge via bndl-resolver (Phase 4) to a ModuleId or external marker.
  • Model ESM live bindings, re-exports, export-star flattening, dynamic import(), and the CJS↔ESM interop boundary (no codegen yet — metadata only).
  • Detect + record import cycles (SCCs); designate entries; load the transitive graph in parallel (thread pool, immutable Send+Sync Module).
  • NON-GOAL: transforms (Phase 6), tree-shaking (Phase 7), emit (Phase 10). Graph is read-only after build.

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File (crates/bndl-graph/src/) Adds
id.rs ModuleId(u32), SymbolId(u32); dense interned indices
module.rs Module{id,path,kind,ast,imports,exports,symbols,has_side_effects}, ModuleKind
record.rs ImportRecord{specifier,resolved:Option<ModuleId>,kind:ImportKind}, ExportRecord{name,local:Option<SymbolId>,reexport:Option<(ModuleId,Atom)>}, ImportKind{Static,Dynamic,Require,ExportStar}
symbol.rs Symbol{name,kind,local}, SymbolKind{Var,Func,Class,Import,Default,Namespace,ExportAlias}, Binding{symbol,is_live}
scan.rs scan_module(&Program,kind)->(Vec<ImportRecord>,Vec<ExportRecord>,Vec<Symbol>,bool) — single Visit pass
cjs.rs static require("x") / module.exports= / exports.k= / Object.defineProperty(exports,..) detection → synthetic records
classify.rs classify(path,ast,ResolveResult)->ModuleKind
build.rs GraphBuilder — BFS frontier + thread pool; ModuleGraph::build(entries,&Resolver)
cycle.rs Tarjan SCC over static edges → cycles:Vec<Vec<ModuleId>>, per-module in_cycle:bool
star.rs flatten_export_star(&mut ModuleGraph) — fixpoint expansion
  • Scan (scan.rs, ADR-08 Visit read-only walk; S2 ECMA-262 §16 ImportEntry/ExportEntry; P10 live-binding semantics): one Visit over Program.items. ModuleItem::Import → one ImportRecord per source + Symbol{kind:Import} per specifier (namespace→Namespace, default→Default). ModuleItem::Export: Named{source:None}→ExportRecord{local}; Named{source:Some}→reexport; All→ImportRecord{ExportStar} (+ExportRecord ns alias if as); Default/Decl→ local export + hoisted symbol. Expr::Import (dynamic) → ImportRecord{Dynamic} only if specifier is a literal string; non-literal → record Unknown flag (graph edge unknown, kept external-safe).
  • CJS scan (cjs.rs; ADR-10 live-binding model; P12 CJS↔ESM interop): only when kind==Cjs. Free require(<lit>)→ImportRecord{Require}; module.exports=/exports.<k>=→ ExportRecord; non-static exports[expr] → set dynamic_exports:true (forces wrapper later). __esModule marker detected for interop.
  • Classify (classify.rs; S8 package.json type field; R1 esbuild module-kind detection): precedence: External/Builtin from ResolveResult → ext .json=Json, .css=Css, known-asset=Asset → else syntax: any ESM import/export ⇒ Esm; else require/module.exports/exports.x ⇒ Cjs; ambiguous (no import/export, no CJS) ⇒ Esm if package.json type:"module"/.mjs, else Cjs. .cjs/.mjs ext is authoritative.
  • Parallel build (build.rs; ADR-20 rayon work-stealing; ADR-07 arena Send+Sync; P1 esbuild parallel-per-file): entries seed a work-stealing pool (std::thread + MPSC, N=available_parallelism). Worker: parse (or cache hit, Phase 13) → classify → scan → resolve each record via shared &Resolver (Arc, interior-mutable cache). New paths get a ModuleId via a Mutex<PathBuf→ModuleId> interner; already-seen paths reuse id (dedupe diamonds). Resolved ids pushed back as frontier. Module is immutable post-construction; graph collected into Vec<Module> ordered by ModuleId. Externals/builtins get a placeholder leaf module, not loaded.
  • Cycles (cycle.rs; B2 §3 SCC/Tarjan; ADR-12 wrap fallback for cyclic modules): Tarjan over static edges (Static+ExportStar; Dynamic is a boundary, excluded). Record SCCs size>1; mark in_cycle. Self-import = trivial cycle.
  • Export-star (star.rs; S2 GetExportedNames; P3 Rollup tree-shaking): post-build fixpoint — each ExportStar edge contributes target's named exports to the re-exporter, minus locally-redeclared names and minus default. Name collisions across multiple stars ⇒ the name is ambiguous (excluded; only an error if actually imported — recorded, not thrown). CJS target of export * resolves to its (snapshot) keys via interop, flagged star_from_cjs.
ModuleId(0)=entryA ──Static──▶ ModuleId(2)=shared ◀──Static── ModuleId(1)=entryB
       │                              │ExportStar ▶ ModuleId(3)=utils
       └──Dynamic──▶ ModuleId(4)=lazy   (chunk boundary; excluded from SCC)

5.3 Deliverable

ModuleGraph::build(entries,&Resolver) returns a complete graph for a multi-entry project: every reachable module loaded once, ModuleKind classified, ImportRecord/ExportRecord/Symbol tables populated, edges resolved to ModuleId, export-star flattened, cycles recorded, entries tagged. bndl graph <entries> prints the dot/JSON graph (ids, kinds, edges, cycles, live-binding flags).

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Diamond import (A→B, A→C, B→D, C→D) yields 4 modules; D has exactly one ModuleId (dedup).
  • export * flattening matches Rollup: re-exporter exposes target's named exports, excludes default + locally-shadowed + ambiguous names.
  • Cycle a↔b recorded as one SCC; both in_cycle=true; build terminates (no infinite frontier).
  • Mixed graph (ESM entry → CJS dep → ESM dep) classifies each ModuleKind correctly; interop boundary flags set.
  • Parallel build is deterministic: ModuleId assignment + module order identical across runs and thread counts (BFS order seeded by sorted resolved specifiers).
  • bndl graph on a 1k-module fixture completes < 400 ms warm; graph output byte-identical run-to-run.

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
diamond/{a,b,c,d}.js 4 modules, D deduped, 4 edges
cycle/{a,b}.js (mutual import) 1 SCC [a,b], both in_cycle, terminates
star/{re,impl}.js (export * from './impl') re exports impl's named, not default
star-ambiguous/{re,x,y}.js (two stars, dup name f) f ambiguous, excluded, recorded
interop/esm-imports-cjs/ dep Cjs, default-import via __toESM flag set
dynamic/main.js (import('./lazy.js')) ImportKind::Dynamic, excluded from SCC
dynamic-expr/main.js (import(x)) Unknown specifier, no edge, no panic
live-binding/{counter,main}.js imported binding is_live=true
malformed: dup-export/ (export {a}; export {a} ) duplicate-export recorded, build continues
malformed: missing-dep/ (import './nope') ResolveError::NotFound attached to record, graph still built
malformed: cjs-dynamic-exports/ (exports[k]=v) dynamic_exports=true

5.6 Pitfalls

# Failure Root cause Debug
1 Diamond dep parsed/built twice path interned by raw string, not realpath → symlinked dup ids key interner on Resolver realpath; log id-assignment per canonical path
2 export * infinite loop mutually-star modules; naive recursion fixpoint with visited-set; iterate to stable, not recurse
3 False cycle through import() dynamic edge counted in SCC exclude ImportKind::Dynamic from Tarjan edge set
4 Live-binding identity lost import treated as value copy, is_live=false mark all ESM imports live (read-only view); CJS require = snapshot
5 Non-deterministic ids HashMap iteration / thread completion order seeds frontier BFS over sorted resolved specifiers; assign id at enqueue, not at parse-done
6 CJS mis-classified ESM TS import type-only file has no runtime import yet scanned as ESM import type contributes type-only record (no edge), classify by runtime items
7 Re-export of default leaked by * spec says export * skips default filter default in star.rs

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • Parallel load is I/O+parse bound; thread pool N=available_parallelism; target ≥ 0.85× linear speedup to 8 cores on cold 1k-module graph.
  • Module immutable + Arc-shared → zero clone of ASTs across workers; arena per module dropped only after graph consumers done.
  • Path interner: single Mutex is the only contention point; amortize with sharded map if profile shows > 5% lock wait.
  • Resolve cache (Phase 4) memoizes specifier→path; expect > 90% hit on node_modules deps in a realistic graph.
  • Scan is one Visit pass O(nodes); no re-walks. Cycle detect O(V+E) Tarjan. Star flatten O(stars × exports), fixpoint bounded by graph depth.

5.8 Edge Cases

  • Self-import (import './self.js') → trivial 1-node SCC, not error.
  • import {} from a module purely for side effects → edge kept, no symbols (pins module).
  • export { x as default } and export default x both → Default symbol.
  • import * as ns + ns.foo → namespace symbol; member access resolved later (Phase 7/10), not in graph.
  • CJS module.exports = require('./x') (re-export pattern) → Require import + whole-object export.
  • ESM importing .json → Json module, single default export (+ named for top-level keys).
  • import('./a' + ext) non-literal → unknown dynamic; do not crash, do not split blindly.
  • TS export type {T} / import type {T} → type-only record, no runtime edge (dropped Phase 6).
  • Builtin (node:fs) / external (configured) → leaf Module, not loaded, edge marked external.

5.9 Failure Analysis

Symptom Likely cause Detection
Build hangs frontier never drains (id reused but re-enqueued) bounded visited-set assert; max-iteration guard logs offending path
Missing module silently resolve error swallowed ResolveError attached to ImportRecord.resolved=None + collected diagnostics, surfaced by CLI
Wrong live-binding semantics downstream is_live set per-specifier incorrectly unit assert: ESM named/default/ns ⇒ live; require ⇒ snapshot
Flaky CI graph snapshot id/order nondeterminism (pitfall 5) run build 3× under varied thread counts, diff serialized graph; must be identical
SCC false positive dynamic edge leaked into Tarjan dump edge set fed to Tarjan; assert no Dynamic

5.10 Operational Notes

  • bndl graph subcommand: --format {dot,json}, --show-cycles, --show-bindings; feeds Phase 7/9 and debugging.
  • Graph serialization is the differential-oracle fixture surface (Phase 13): stable JSON (sorted keys, id-ordered modules) diffed vs golden.
  • Telemetry: per-build counters — modules loaded, cache hits, externals, cycles, parallel-worker utilization; logged at --verbose.
  • Cross-crate contract: graph is immutable to all later phases (transform/shake/chunk read only); any mutation goes through a rebuild, preserving determinism + cache invariants.
  • Diagnostics (duplicate export, ambiguous star, missing dep) collected non-fatally; build exits nonzero only if an unrecoverable resolve failure on a statically-required module.

18. Phase 6 — Transform pipeline (TypeStrip, JSX, lowering, define)

bndl-transform: AST → AST rewrites. VisitMut driver, deterministic pass order, TransformContext. Four passes: TypeStrip, JsxTransform, Lowering, Define. Every pass span-preserving (maps via #265). NON-GOAL: type checking (#42) — TS types stripped, never validated.

6.1 Objectives

  • TransformPass trait + VisitMut driver; LOCKED order: Define → TypeStrip → JsxTransform → Lowering. Define-before-shake so dead branches collapse; TypeStrip before JSX so <T>x casts gone before <jsx>; lowering last (operates on fully-desugared JS).
  • TypeStrip: drop TS-only ModuleItem/Stmt/Expr/type-annotation nodes; keep value spans intact for source maps; collapse enum/namespace/param-properties to JS.
  • JsxTransform: classic createElement + automatic jsx/jsxs/Fragment (JsxRuntime::Classic|Automatic); key extraction, spread children, <>…</>.
  • Lowering: only what Target lacks — optional chaining, nullish coalescing/assign, logical assign, class fields/private, numeric separators, exponent, etc.
  • Define: inline process.env.NODE_ENV-style globals → literal Expr; produce statically-foldable conditions feeding the shaker (Phase 7).
  • Determinism: identical (AST, opts) → identical output AST + span deltas.

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Module (crates/bndl-transform/src/) Adds
lib.rs TransformPass, TransformContext<'a>{target,defines,syntax,jsx_runtime}, Target, DefineMap, JsxRuntime, transform(p:&mut Program, ctx:&mut TransformContext) driver
driver.rs fixed pass vec; each pass = dyn TransformPass; sequential visit_mut(p,ctx); reports TransformStats{passes_run, nodes_removed, helpers_used}
typestrip.rs TypeStrip; matches TsTypeAnn, TsInterface, TsTypeAlias, import type, as/satisfies/!, decorators(emit-or-drop), enum→IIFE/object, namespace→IIFE, ctor param-props→assignments
jsx.rs JsxTransform; JsxRuntime switch; element→createElement(tag,props,...children) or jsx(tag,{...,children})/jsxs; Fragment/React.Fragment; key hoist; spread/{...x} props; entity/whitespace JSXText folding
lowering/*.rs per-feature lowerers gated by Target: optchain.rs, nullish.rs, logical_assign.rs, class_fields.rs, private.rs, numeric.rs, exponent.rs, spread.rs
helpers.rs injected helper registry (__spread, __decorate, __privateGet...); dedup per chunk; emitted by codegen (Phase 10), not here
define.rs Define; member-path match (a.b.c), word-boundary ident match; replace w/ parsed literal Expr; never inside binding/decl positions
  • Atom interning reused for synthesized idents (_a, helper names) via a per-TransformContext gensym counter → stable, collision-checked against ScopeTree in Phase 8 (ADR-06 Atom interning; P7 SWC Atom).
  • Each rewrite copies the original node's Span onto the synthesized root node; new sub-nodes get the nearest enclosing span → map fidelity (ADR-18 source-map composition through #265; P11 Babel transform pass model).
  • Class-field lowering (S4 TypeScript class fields; ADR-08 VisitMut in-place rewrite): instance fields → constructor head (after super()), statics → post-class assignments; #priv → WeakMap/closure-keyed accessor via __privateGet/Set.
// signature only — no body
pub fn transform(p: &mut Program, ctx: &mut TransformContext);

Pass-ordering contract (each pass runs visit_mut on the full Program in sequence):

Order Pass VisitMut impl Why this position
1 Define Replace global ident/member-path with parsed literal Expr Must run before JSX/lowering see the replaced node
2 TypeStrip Erase all TS-only syntax nodes; keep value semantics unchanged TS annotations invisible to later passes
3 JsxTransform Rewrite JSX elements → createElement calls or jsx-runtime calls JSX gone before lowering applies target transforms
4 Lowering Lower optional-chaining, nullish, class fields, async to Target Operates on clean JS AST, no TS/JSX residue

6.3 Deliverable

bndl-transform lowering TS/JSX/modern JS → Target-level JS in-place on Program; reachable via bndl-cli transform <file> --target <t> --jsx <classic|automatic>; emits transformed AST + span deltas consumed by codegen. cargo test -p bndl-transform green; before/after fixtures + map-preservation check pass.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
TypeStrip residue 0 TS-only nodes in output AST (asserted by walk)
JSX both runtimes classic + automatic outputs match esbuild AST shape on fixture set
Lowering coverage every gated feature lowered when target < feature_min, untouched when ≥
Span monotonicity every output node span ⊆ some input span; no (0,0) synthetic spans on value nodes
Determinism 100 reruns byte-identical printed output (after Phase 10)
Throughput ≥ 12 MB/s single-thread on TS-heavy corpus (transform-only)

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expects
typestrip/enum_const.ts → .out.js const enum→inlined literals; enum→IIFE bidirectional map
typestrip/param_props.ts ctor private x → this.x = x
jsx/automatic_key_spread.tsx <a key={k} {...p}/> → jsx("a",{...p},k) (key as 3rd arg)
jsx/fragment.tsx <>…</> → jsxs(Fragment,{children:[…]})
lower/optchain_nullish.ts --target es2015 a?.b ?? c → ternary + temp var

Malformed jsx/unenabled.js (JSX, syntax.jsx=false) → ParseErrorKind::JsxNotEnabled upstream; define/bad_replacement (unparseable value) → config error, no panic

6.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
enum reverse-map dropped only emitting forward E.A=0, not E[0]="A" diff against tsc emit; assert both directions
JSX key left in props (automatic) not hoisting key out of prop object inspect jsx 3rd arg; spec: key is positional, NOT a prop
Optional-chain short-circuit lost lowering a?.b.c per-link instead of whole-chain → double eval / wrong undefined boundary trace temp-var assignment count; one temp per chain head
Define inside shorthand/binding replacing {NODE_ENV} or let NODE_ENV match only reference positions; skip Pat/decl idents
Class-field this before super() hoisting field init above super() in derived class → TDZ throw place inits immediately after super() call site
Decorator order reversed applying decorators bottom-up vs spec top-down composition compare __decorate arg order to TS

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • Single combined bottom-up VisitMut per pass; avoid re-walking — 4 passes = 4 traversals, not N. #[inline] hot match arms.
  • Reuse arena allocation for synthesized nodes; no per-node heap alloc on the JS-only fast path (TypeStrip is mostly removals → Vec::retain).
  • Define precompiles a HashMap<Path,Expr> + a max-segment length to early-reject member chains.
  • JSXText entity decoding: single pass, reuse scratch String; cache Atom for Fragment/runtime idents.
  • Lowering skips entirely when target == EsNext (no-op pass elided from driver vec).

6.8 Edge Cases

  • export type {T} / import type fully elided incl. side-effect-free source pin removal; import {type A, b} → keep b only.
  • TS declare/.d.ts modules → emit empty program (value-free).
  • JSX namespaced/member.tag (<a.b:c/>), boolean prop shorthand, dangerouslySetInnerHTML passthrough.
  • Nullish-assign a ??= b must read a once (member/computed key eval once).
  • #priv in obj (ergonomic brand check) lowering distinct from access.
  • Define value containing identifiers (__DEV__ → import.meta.env.DEV) re-parsed in context, not string-spliced.
  • Numeric separator + BigInt (1_000n) strip underscores, keep n.

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Synthetic node missing span debug-assert in driver: every node span ≠ default reject in tests; CI gate
Helper name collides w/ user ident Phase 8 Renamer collision check gensym retry w/ counter bump
Unsupported TS construct (rare) exhaustive match arm unreachable! guarded by feature flag emit diagnostic, fall back to pass-through (no panic)
Define replacement loops path replacement re-scanned one-shot replace, no fixpoint on Define
Lowering produces invalid target node post-pass target-conformance walk (test-only) fail fixture, not silent

6.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: bndl transform <f> [--target] [--jsx classic|automatic] [--jsx-import-source] [--define K=V]...; prints transformed JS (+ .map if --sourcemap).
  • Pass order is LOCKED in driver.rs; changing it is a breaking semantic change — document in ADR.
  • TransformStats logged at --verbose (helpers used → Phase 10 dedup; nodes removed → sanity).
  • Helpers are named here, emitted by bndl-codegen (Phase 10); transform only records helpers_used: HashSet<&'static str>.
  • Cacheable: (ast_hash, target, defines_hash, jsx_runtime) → Artifact::Transformed (Phase 13, BLAKE3 CacheKey).

19. Phase 7 — Tree-shaking (symbol reachability + side effects)

Crate: bndl-treeshake. Consumes ModuleGraph (post Phase 5/6), emits a ReachableSet + mutates the graph to drop dead symbols/statements. Soundness-first: drop only what is provably unobservable.

7.1 Objectives

  • Build SymbolGraph: per-SymbolId def + use edges, intra- and cross-module, threaded through ImportRecord/ExportRecord/re-exports.
  • Reachability worklist from entry exports → minimal live SymbolId set; mark live statements/exports/imports.
  • SideEffect analysis: top-level effectful stmts, sideEffects:false package contract, /*#__PURE__*/, getters/proxies treated Unknown→Impure.
  • Eliminate: dead statements, unused named/default exports, unused imports, dead re-export specifiers, unreachable export-star members.
  • Invariant: every observable effect (assignments to globals, calls, throws, getters) preserved; over-removal is a correctness bug, not a win.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File (bndl-treeshake/src/) Contents
symbol_graph.rs SymbolGraph{ edges: Vec<(SymbolId,SymbolId)>, defs: FxHashMap<SymbolId,StmtRef>, sym_effect: Vec<SideEffect> }; StmtRef{module:ModuleId, item_idx:u32}.
sideeffect.rs enum SideEffect{Pure,Impure,Unknown}; analyze_stmt(&Stmt)->SideEffect; pure_call(&CallExpr)->bool (PURE annotation/known-pure list).
reach.rs struct Reachability{ live: BitSet<SymbolId>, live_stmts: FxHashSet<StmtRef>, work: Vec<SymbolId> }; pub fn shake(graph:&mut ModuleGraph, entries:&[ModuleId]) -> ReachableSet.
eliminate.rs prune(graph:&mut ModuleGraph, &ReachableSet): drops ModuleItems, ExportRecords, ImportRecords not live.
report.rs RemovalReport{ in_bytes, out_bytes, removed_symbols, removed_stmts }; emitted for bndl analyze.

Algorithm — shake (P3 Rollup symbol-level tree-shaking; ADR-11 symbol-level granularity; B2 §9.2/9.4 dataflow liveness; B3 DCE):

  1. Seed: for each entry, push every exported SymbolId (and the namespace symbol if import *) onto work. Pin every top-level stmt with analyze_stmt != Pure (module-level side effects → mark module live, but only effectful stmts).
  2. Propagate: pop s; if already in live skip; insert; for each (s, d) def→use edge push d; resolve s's defining stmt → mark live_stmts; follow its referenced symbols (incl. imported bindings → resolve through ImportRecord.resolved + matching ExportRecord; re-export chains followed transitively; export-star expands to the concrete re-exported member only when its name is requested).
  3. Effect closure: a live stmt drags in symbols it references; an impure unreferenced stmt stays but does NOT force its writes-only symbols live unless read elsewhere.
  4. Result: ReachableSet{ live: BitSet, live_stmts }.

Side-effect classification (S8 sideEffects package.json field; P3 Rollup /*#__PURE__*/ contract; ADR-11 over-removal = correctness bug): sideEffects:false (from ResolveResult.side_effects) ⇒ unreferenced module fully droppable; glob patterns (sideEffects:["*.css"]) matched per-module. /*#__PURE__*/ before a CallExpr/NewExpr ⇒ Pure if result unused. Bare member access on possibly-getter object ⇒ Unknown→treat Impure.

7.3 Deliverable

TreeShaker removing provably-dead code over the fixture suite; bndl build --analyze prints a RemovalReport; a soundness harness proving identical runtime observable behavior pre/post-shake; removal-ratio table vs Rollup.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Soundness fixtures: post-shake bundle output (executed under #126 oracle / Node) emits byte-identical stdout/effect-trace to unshaken — zero divergence across the suite.
  • Removal ratio within ±5% of Rollup on the shared fixture set (fixtures/treeshake/*).
  • Unused export export const x with no importer ⇒ removed; sideEffects:false lib import of one fn ⇒ siblings dropped.
  • Determinism: identical ReachableSet.live bitset bytes across 100 runs.
  • Performance: shake ≤ 8 ms per 1k modules single-thread.

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
unused_export.js export const used=1; export const dead=2; only used imported dead removed, used kept
pure_call.js /*#__PURE__*/ foo(); const y=bar(); y unused both dropped if bar pure-annotated; else bar() kept
side_effect_module.js import w/ sideEffects:false, only a used b,c symbols + their stmts removed
reexport_chain.js export {x} from './m' x unused downstream re-export specifier + source binding pruned
export_star_partial.js export * from './big', 1 name used only used member retained, rest dead
getter_effect.js obj.prop where prop is getter NOT removed (Unknown→Impure)
malformed: top_level_throw.js throw new Error() at module top, unimported export throw stmt PINNED (observable), export still droppable

7.6 Pitfalls

Pitfall Root cause Debug
Dropping a getter access bare obj.x looks pure but triggers user getter/Proxy trace sym_effect; force Impure on member access of non-literal object
Live binding mutation lost shaker removes a mutated import view (counter++ in dep) check def→use edge crosses ImportRecord; preserve mutation-visibility per spec
Over-pruning re-export export-star member dropped but reached via dynamic ns[k] dynamic namespace access ⇒ retain all star members; flag ns_dynamic
sideEffects:false lie lib declares false but has top-level polyfill honor contract (it's a contract); document; offer --no-treeshake-sideeffects override
Class static-block effects static { sideEffect() } not seen as top-level classify static block as Impure, pin enclosing class symbol if reachable
TDZ reorder concatenation later reorders; shaker assumes original order keep StmtRef.item_idx stable; don't reorder in this phase

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • live as fixed BitSet<SymbolId>; worklist dedup via the bitset itself — no per-pop hash lookup.
  • Symbol edges built once during graph scan (Phase 5), not recomputed; SymbolGraph is Send+Sync immutable post-build → reachability parallelizable per entry, union the bitsets.
  • Avoid quadratic re-export following: memoize resolved (ModuleId,Atom)→SymbolId in FxHashMap.
  • Effect analysis is O(top-level stmts), not full-AST — only scans module items + their direct call/member exprs.

7.8 Edge Cases

  • Circular re-exports (a→b→a) — visited-set guard, no infinite loop.
  • export { x as default } + separate export default — distinct ExportRecords, independent liveness.
  • Dynamic import() target — whole module's used exports live; can't statically narrow unless arg is literal (then narrow).
  • import './polyfill' (effect-only, no bindings) — always kept (effect import), never pruned.
  • Reassigned export (export let n; n=…) — both decl + assignment live if n reachable.
  • Namespace import partially used statically then spread — spread ⇒ all members live.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Removed observable effect soundness oracle diff (#126) bisect removed StmtRefs; reclassify offending stmt Impure; CI hard-fail
Cross-module edge missing reachable set too small, runtime undefined assert every ImportRecord.resolved has matching ExportRecord; else ResolveError upstream
Hash/output nondeterminism 100-run bitset diff enforce stable SymbolId ordering from graph build
Pruned dynamic-access member runtime prop is undefined conservative: dynamic namespace/computed access disables member-level pruning for that ns

7.10 Operational Notes

  • Config: treeshake: bool (default true for build, off for dev server), treeshake.module_side_effects: "auto"|true|false, treeshake.annotations: bool (honor /*#__PURE__*/).
  • bndl analyze surfaces RemovalReport (in/out bytes, removed symbol count, per-module dead %); bndl graph --dead lists eliminated symbols with reasons.
  • Dev server skips shaking (ESM-native, on-demand) — shake only on build; cache key (bndl-cache) includes treeshake options so toggling invalidates artifacts.
  • Logging: RUST_LOG=bndl_treeshake=debug dumps worklist trace + per-symbol effect verdict for soundness triage.

20. Phase 8 — Scope analysis + scope hoisting (module concatenation)

Builds ScopeTree + Renamer in bndl-codegen, then concatenates eligible ESM modules into one shared scope (Rollup/esbuild-style) and wraps the rest in memoized runtime functions. Consumes ModuleGraph + ReachableSet (Phase 7), feeds Phase 10 codegen. Live-binding identity is the load-bearing invariant.

8.1 Objectives

  • Build ScopeTree: module/function/block scopes; record every binding (var/let/const/function/class/param/import/catch) + every reference; TDZ-relevant flags.
  • Renamer: assign collision-free global names across concatenated modules; respect captures, reserved words, exported names, with/eval hazards.
  • Scope hoisting: concatenate eligible ESM modules → single scope; rewrite import refs to direct symbol refs; preserve declaration order honoring cross-module deps.
  • Wrapper fallback: wrap ineligible (CJS, cycle-needing-lazy-init, eval/with-containing, dynamic-import target boundary) in __esm/__commonJS memoized fns; preserve live bindings throughout (mutated exports stay visible at all import sites post-rename; namespace objects reflect updates).

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

Files (all in bndl-codegen, per section 10):

File Contents
scope/tree.rs ScopeTree, Scope, ScopeId, ScopeKind, BindingInfo, RefInfo
scope/build.rs ScopeBuilder: Visit — two-pass hoist + bind walk per module
scope/rename.rs Renamer, RenameMap, reserved-word set, top-level slot allocator
hoist/eligibility.rs WrapKind, classify_wrap(module, graph) -> WrapKind
hoist/concat.rs Concatenator — splice ASTs, rewrite import refs, order modules
hoist/binding_map.rs BindingMap: (ModuleId, Atom) -> SymbolId -> renamed Atom

Key types:

pub enum ScopeKind { Module, Function, Block, Catch, Class, With }
pub struct BindingInfo { sym: SymbolId, name: Atom, kind: BindKind, hoisted: bool,
                         is_const: bool, captured: bool, declared_span: Span }
pub struct Scope { id: ScopeId, parent: Option<ScopeId>, kind: ScopeKind,
                   bindings: FxHashMap<Atom, BindingInfo>, refs: Vec<RefInfo>,
                   has_eval: bool, has_with: bool }
pub struct ScopeTree { scopes: Vec<Scope>, node_scope: FxHashMap<NodeId, ScopeId> }
pub enum WrapKind { Hoisted, EsmWrapped, CjsWrapped }  // __esm vs __commonJS

Algorithms (P4 Rollup scope hoisting; ADR-12 module concatenation; B4 Ch.5 symbol tables + scopes; B3 §8/9 scalar opts):

  1. Build (scope/build.rs): pass-A collect hoisted (var+function to nearest function/module scope); pass-B bind lexical decls + resolve each ref to declaring scope by upward walk; flag captured when ref scope ≠ decl function scope; set has_eval/has_with on enclosing scope and all ancestors-to-function.
  2. Eligibility (hoist/eligibility.rs; ADR-12 wrap fallback criteria): Hoisted iff ESM ∧ no eval/with ∧ not in lazy-init cycle ∧ not a CJS-interop consumer needing snapshot. Else EsmWrapped (ESM but lazy) or CjsWrapped.
  3. Rename (scope/rename.rs; ADR-14 determinism contract; R2 Rollup chunkAssignment stable order): deterministic order = module topo index, then binding declared_span. Top-level binding of a hoisted module → fresh global slot; collision/reserved/exported-name conflict → suffix $N. Nested scopes keep names unless they shadow a chosen global (then rename the inner). Build BindingMap.
  4. Concatenate (hoist/concat.rs; P4 module concatenation; ADR-15 injected helpers): order hoisted modules by dependency (deps-before-dependents; cycle members emitted in graph order); strip import/export statements; replace imported-ident refs via BindingMap (namespace import → synthesized object literal of live getters or direct refs); splice all bodies into one Program. Wrap ineligible modules (ADR-10 live-binding interop model; P12 CJS↔ESM interop helpers): emit var mod = __esm({ "id"() { <body> } }) (ESM lazy) or __commonJS (CJS); rewrite consumers to __toESM(mod()).

8.3 Deliverable

emit path produces a concatenated Program (hoisted modules inlined, wrapped modules as runtime-fn entries) with a populated BindingMap; bndl analyze --hoist <entry> prints per-module WrapKind + rename map; runtime-behavior suite + wrapper-fallback fixtures pass.

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Hoist rate (typical ESM app, no CJS) ≥95% modules Hoisted
Rename correctness 0 binding collisions; 0 unresolved refs over fixture suite
Live-binding fixtures 100% pass (mutated export visible at all sites)
Determinism byte-identical rename map across 50 runs
Wrapper fallback every CJS/cyclic/eval module wrapped, memoized once (single init)

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
hoist/two_module.js both inlined, one scope, no import left
hoist/name_collision.js (both define x) one x, other x$1
hoist/live_binding.js (counter mutated post-import) reads observe mutation
hoist/namespace_import.js (import * as ns) ns.foo resolves to live ref/getter
hoist/reexport_chain.js re-exported symbol mapped to original decl
wrap/cjs_module.js __commonJS, __toESM at consumer
